of uniform make-up."],"tags":["not-comparable"]},{"glosses":["In the same state of matter."],"tags":["not-comparable"],"topics":["chemistry","natural-sciences","physical-sciences"]},{"glosses":["In any of several technical senses uniform; scalable; having its behavior or form determined by, or the same as, its behavior on or form at a smaller component (of its domain of definition, of itself, etc.).","Of polynomials, functions, equations, systems of equations, or linear maps:","Such that all its nonzero terms have the same degree."],"raw_tags":["of a polynomial"],"tags":["not-comparable"],"topics":["algebra","mathematics","sciences"]},{"glosses":["In any of several technical senses uniform;
